# Commands · P · part 12

1,492 entries, pr-description to prd-status. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pr-description**: - PRは現在のブランチから作成されている物を利用する - 既存のPR説明文が少ない場合は上書きすること - 必要に応じてブランチ名の数字部分 `***/123` のissue (例の場合#123) の説明文やコメントに設計があるため、参照して適切な概要等を記載する - 関連Issueには上記Issue番号を記載し、Close対象とする - スクリーンショットや動画は手動で貼り付ける為このコマンドでは無視する - タイトルも必要に応じて変更する ([UNILORN/generative-commit-message-for-ai-tool](https://github.com/UNILORN/generative-commit-message-for-ai-tool/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pr-description.md) · ★2)

- **pr-dev**: Everyday finalise → ONE merge-ready PR to dev (→ TestPyPI on merge). Take a feature/fix/chore branch from "code+tests done" to a PR with docs reconciliation folded in as a completion criterion (CHANGELOG, FAQ touch-up, `docs/reference/test-catalog.md`). Green locally first, apply the batch-cohesion rule, open the table-shaped PR, hand off needs-verification. May merge to dev only (--merge + confirm). Never master/tag/release/deploy. ([littlebearapps/untether](https://github.com/littlebearapps/untether/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pr-dev.md) · ★77)

- **pr-distill**: 1. **Heuristics First**: Run heuristic pattern matching before AI analysis. Heuristics are fast and deterministic. 2. **Confidence Requires Evidence**: Never mark a change as "safe to skip" without pattern match or AI justification. 3. **Surface Uncertainty**: Low-confidence cases → categorize as "uncertain". Humans decide ambiguous cases. 4. **Preserve Context**: Include enough diff context for r ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/pr-distill.md) · ★13)
- **pr-distill-bless**: 1. **User Confirmation Required**: Never bless patterns automatically. User must explicitly invoke this command. 2. **Validate Pattern ID**: Pattern must match validation rules (lowercase, hyphens, 2-50 chars). 3. **Warn on Overwrite**: If pattern already exists, warn and confirm before updating. 4. **Persistence Is Immediate**: Once blessed, pattern affects all future distillations in this projec ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/pr-distill-bless.md) · ★13)

- **prd-stage-03**: You are a product structuring specialist. Your job is to normalize raw material into a clean concept document. You capture WHAT and WHY — zero HOW. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-03.md))
- **prd-stage-04**: You are a mechanism extraction specialist. Your job is to decompose the structured concept into every discrete functional mechanism, classify them, and map their dependencies. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-04.md))
- **prd-stage-05**: You are the deterministic scaffolding engine. This is the most important stage. You apply the 7-question framework to every mechanism, classifying every process step as WALL, DOOR, or ROOM. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-05.md))
- **prd-stage-07**: You are a phase sequencing specialist. Your job is to split the complete spec into buildable phases with token budgets, file sandboxes, and forced build orders. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-07.md))
- **prd-stage-08**: You are a verification protocol specialist. Your job is to inject three tiers of verification checkpoints into the phased build orders, producing self-verifying build units. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-08.md))
- **prd-stage-09**: You are a verification architecture specialist. Your job is to configure an independent verification agent (Agent B) that audits builder output after each phase using git diff as ground truth. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-09.md))
- **prd-stage-10**: You are the output rendering engine. Your job is pure serialization — take all decisions from Stages 0-9 and render them into a copy-paste-ready file package. Zero design decisions remain at this point. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-10.md))
- **prd-stage-10-v2**: You are the output rendering engine. Your job is pure serialization — take all decisions from Stages 0-9 and render them into a copy-paste-ready file package. Zero design decisions remain at this point. ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/prd-stage-10-v2.md))

- **prd-start**: Full interactive PRD pipeline - boilerplate through Agent OS (steps 0-3B) ([digisurfsome/Greptacular](https://github.com/digisurfsome/Greptacular/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/prd-start.md))
